# AArch64's own intrinsics (llvm.aarch64.*, as Rust's std::arch and C's
# arm_neon.h and arm_acle.h make them): what tests/intrinsics.rs does with
# each (see there for the form of a line). The machine is an M1 (Armv8.5
# with crypto, SHA-3, dot products, FP16, FHM, RDM, FCMA, JSCVT and
# FRINTTS); what it lacks is skipped.

# --- NEON: integers, lane by lane
test ; llvm.aarch64.neon.abs.{0} ; 0(0) ; nint64 | i64
test ; llvm.aarch64.neon.sqabs.{0} ; 0(0) ; nint64 | i32 | i64
test ; llvm.aarch64.neon.sqneg.{0} ; 0(0) ; nint64 | i32 | i64

test ; llvm.aarch64.neon.sqrdmulh.laneq.{0}.{1} ; 0(0, 1, i32 3) ; <4 x i16>, <8 x i16> | <4 x i32>, <4 x i32>
test ; llvm.aarch64.neon.sqrdmlah.{0} ; 0(0, 0, 0) ; <4 x i16> | <8 x i16> | <2 x i32> | <4 x i32> | i32

skip ; llvm.aarch64.sys ; system instructions (SYS), which a user program cannot run
